      "content": "# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main Track** (D043, 用户指定)\n- ARR cycle: June 2026 (deadline ~June 15, 待确认具体日期)\n- 格式: ACL 系列模板 (acl.sty), 8 页正文 + unlimited appendix\n- Note: ARR May cycle 已过；June cycle 对齐 EMNLP 2026 commitment deadline\n\n## Narrative Structure (D130: Discovery Paper Reframe)\n\n**主线**：Detection Phase Transition Discovery。我们发现 canary-downstream 因果检测能力在 α_c≈0.50 附近经历 near-discontinuous onset (sigmoid R²=0.969, k≥200)，且 detection threshold 由 per-generation mixing ratio 决定而非 cumulative contamination。Multi-method 分析是发现这一现象的工具，不是贡献本身。\n**四根支柱**：(1) Detection phase transition = C1 核心发现；(2) S×R 理论框架 + 5-regime 预测 = C2 机制解释；(3) Multi-method diagnostic toolkit (MMCT) = C3 方法贡献（降级为工具）；(4) Four-layer taxonomy + early cessation + data strategy = C4 实践贡献。\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：C1 (phase transition discovery) 领衔 → C2 (theoretical framework) → C3 (multi-method toolkit) → C4 (practical applications)。\n\n## Core Claims (D130: 4 Contributions)\n\n> **Claim mapping (D130)**: D130 重组了贡献结构。旧 Claim 1 (Granger precedence) -> C1/C3; 旧 Claim 2 (regime-dependent) -> C1/C2; 旧 Claim 3 (method sensitivity) -> C3; 旧 Claim 4 (intervention) -> C4。下文 Key Narrative Constraints 中引用 \"Claim 1/2/3/4\" 仍指旧编号，按此映射解读。\n\n**C1: Detection Phase Transition (核心发现)** — Canary-downstream 因果检测能力在 alpha_c approx 0.476 附近经历 steep onset (at T=10; steepness T-dependent per C2 Power Theory)。Sigmoid fit R^2=0.969, steepness k>=200, transition width Delta-alpha approx 0.02。PermG 从 0/8 FDR-sig (alpha<=0.45; sub-threshold signal present: TY=7/8 at α=0.45, fdr_p gradient 0.059–0.094) 跳至 6/8 (alpha=0.50)，cross-seed validated (s42/s43/s44)。**核心 insight**: detection threshold 是 per-generation mixing ratio alpha，NOT cumulative contamination C(alpha,G)=1-(1-alpha)^G。alpha=0.45 cumulative=99.75% vs alpha=0.50=99.90% (0.15pp diff)，但 PermG 0/8 vs 6/8。形式化: Delta-h(g) proportional to alpha * d_TV(p_g^model, p^real)。Cross-architecture: PermG fwd >=6/8 in 10/14 combinations (Pythia-6.9B/1.4B/410M, GPT-2m, OPT-1.3B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, C4-Pythia-1B; exceptions: 1.4B s42=2/8, SmolLM3=3/8)。Four-layer detection taxonomy: (1) effective_rank/dimensionality collapse alpha>=0.10, (2) distributional variance alpha>=0.25, (3) PermG causal coupling alpha>=0.50, (4) AC1 dynamical stability alpha>=0.50 (suggestive, T=11 insufficient)。Onset interval [0.45, 0.50] from 13-point alpha-sweep (alpha=0.25/0.30/0.35/0.40/0.45 all 0/8; alpha=0.25 borderline FDR p=0.053)。**CSD Dissociation (Track A v2, D138)**: Canary CSD 存在 (AR(1) τ=0.79-0.82, variance τ=0.73-0.88, p<0.001), downstream CSD 缺失 (τ=0.03-0.30, p>0.19) → canary-triggered cascade 机制。Canary 二阶渐进失稳 + downstream 一阶突变 → blind zone 内无 lead-lag 可检测。**4-stage cascade**: Structure (α=0.10, eff_rank) → Canary destabilizes (α≥0.25, CSD) → Causal lead-lag emerges (α≥0.50, PermG) → Full cascade (α≥0.75)。CSD 整合入 C1 narrative arc，不独立成 C5（8 页限制）。措辞约束 (D139 更新): \"detection phase transition\" / \"sharp onset\" / \"steep onset\" 允许; \"near-discontinuous onset\" 降级（a045_s43 TY=7/8 证明信号连续存在）; \"first-order phase transition\" / \"phase transition\" (physical sense) 禁止。\n\n**C2: Theoretical Framework (机制解释)** — S(alpha) x R(alpha) 竞争模型: Detectability proportional to S(alpha) x R(alpha)。S = signal strength (monotonic increasing with alpha); R = temporal resolution (non-monotonic, drops at alpha approx 0.60 when relaxation time tau_relax(alpha) matches observation interval)。Predicts 4 regimes: (I) Blind zone alpha<0.45 — S too weak; (II) Onset alpha approx 0.45-0.50 — S crosses detection threshold; (III) Sync dip alpha approx 0.55-0.65 — R drops, tau_relax approx Delta-t, zero-lag synchronization destroys PermG lag structure; (IV) Detection plateau alpha>=0.70 — S dominates despite low R。Experimental validation: non-monotonic PermG dose-response 0/8 -> 6/8 -> 0-3/8 -> 5-6/8 matches prediction (dual-seed confirmed at alpha=0.60: s42=3/8, s43=0/8)。Cross-disciplinary connections: hormesis (Calabrese 2003, non-monotonic dose-response), zero-lag GC failure (Deshpande 2010, sync regime), critical speeding up (Titus 2020, near-threshold dynamics)。**D133 Statistical Power Theory (Directive 15)**: S x R 模型有 statistical power 理论基础。alpha_c 不是 physical phase transition 而是 power threshold：Power(alpha, T) = Phi(S(alpha) x R(alpha) x sqrt(T) / sigma)。当 Power 超过检测阈值时产生 \"detection phase transition\"。**3 个 T=20 可检验预测**：(1) alpha_c LEFT-SHIFT（更多数据 → 更低检测下界）; (2) transition SMOOTHING（power 跨所有 alpha 提升 → 不那么 step-like）; (3) 不同方法有不同 power curve → 不同 alpha_c（PermG vs MMCT）。**a045_s43 TY=7/8 Smoking Gun (D139)**: α=0.45 PermG=0/8 但 TY=7/8 — 因果信号在 α=0.45 已存在（TY 检出 7/8），但 PermG power 不够。直接验证预测(3): method-dependent α_c（TY α_c ∈ [0.30, 0.45], PermG α_c ∈ [0.45, 0.50]）。fdr_p gradient: s43 min=0.059 (距阈值 18%), s42 min=0.091 (距 82%) — power boundary 处 seed 敏感。T=20 实验 RUNNING (t20_a050/a060/a075/a100 + t20_a045 待启) 直接验证预测(1)和(2)。Gu et al. (2025, NeurIPS Spotlight, arXiv:2505.18091) data mixing phase transitions 提供理论类比。\n\n**C3: Multi-Method Diagnostic Toolkit (降级为发现工具)** — MMCT (5 methods: naive_fwd + diff_fwd + threshold_onset + PermG + TY) 是发现 C1 的工具，非核心贡献本身。PermG null FPR=0/24 across 3 seeds (唯一 zero-FPR method)。K>=3 consensus 10/14 architecture-seed combinations。Method sensitivity 作为 supporting evidence: naive 100% sig -> differenced PermG 25% sig; 同一 test at alpha=1.0 yields 24/24 FDR-sig vs alpha<=0.45 yields 0/8。三层方法论陷阱: (Layer 1) naive Pearson 一切 \"显著\"; (Layer 2) 差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道 (8/8 -> 0/8) while diversity preserved (24/24 -> 24/24); (Layer 3) TY d_max sensitivity 0% <-> 94% spurious rate。Evidence from旧 Claims 1-3 仍然有效: Granger-causal precedence at alpha>=0.50 (旧C1), regime-dependent detectability (旧C2), method selection sensitivity (旧C3)。这些在 D130 下从 \"core contributions\" 降级为 \"evidence supporting the discovery\"。详细约束见下文 Key Narrative Constraints。\n\n**C4: Practical Applications (实践贡献)** — (a) Four-layer detection taxonomy (见 C1): 从业者按 alpha 水平选用不同检测层; (b) Early cessation: 15% token_entropy threshold 触发，比 reactive 早 1 代, ppl reduction 58.3% (bootstrap 95% CI: [28.5%, 63.7%]); Prevention paradox — 有效干预 K>=3=0/8 (gen 1 截断 collapse 轨迹, Granger 丧失统计基础), reactive 8/8 全过; (c) Data strategy warning: replace mode PermG=6/8 vs accumulate mode 0/8 (V-shaped entropy recovery breaks lead-lag), dual-seed robust (s42+s43); (d) bf16 precision sensitivity: gpt2m_s43 bf16=0/8 vs fp32=6/8 — bf16 completely eliminates causal signal。Scope: early cessation confirmed at alpha>=0.75; framing = \"detection + timely cessation\" not \"active recovery\"。Prevention paradox insight 是方法论发现的一部分——有效干预在统计上不可检测。\n\n## Story Spine (D130: Discovery Paper)\n\n- **Problem**: Iterative training on model-generated data causes progressive model collapse, but the fundamental question — **at what contamination level does causal detection become possible?** — remains unanswered. Existing work (Du et al., SIGMA, DCES) uses ad hoc methods without systematic statistical validation across contamination regimes, and no work has characterized how detectability depends on the mixing ratio. Scheffer et al. (2009, 2012) established early-warning indicator frameworks for critical transitions in ecology/climate, but these have not been applied to model collapse.\n\n- **Discovery**: We discover a **detection phase transition** — a near-discontinuous onset of causal detectability at alpha_c approx 0.476 (sigmoid R^2=0.969, k>=200, Delta-alpha approx 0.02). Below this threshold, canary-downstream causal coupling is statistically undetectable despite ongoing collapse; above it, PermG reliably detects 6/8 metric pairs. The transition is governed by per-generation mixing ratio alpha, NOT cumulative contamination — 99.75% vs 99.90% cumulative yields 0/8 vs 6/8 detection.\n\n- **Mechanism**: We propose the S(alpha) x R(alpha) competition model explaining the non-monotonic detectability pattern. Signal strength S monotonically increases with alpha, but temporal resolution R drops at alpha approx 0.60 when relaxation time matches observation interval. The product S x R predicts 4 regimes (blind -> onset -> sync dip -> detection) validated by PermG dose-response across 13 alpha values and 7+ architectures.\n\n- **Tool**: Multi-method diagnostic toolkit (MMCT, 5 independent methods) enables the discovery. PermG achieves 0/24 null FPR. The toolkit reveals systematic method sensitivity: naive correlation finds 100% significant, differenced PermG retains 25%. MMCT is the discovery instrument, not the discovery itself.\n\n- **Implications**: Four-layer detection taxonomy for practitioners, early cessation protocol (58.3% ppl reduction), data strategy warning (accumulate mode defeats detection), bf16 precision sensitivity, and prevention paradox.\n\n- **Headline result**: \"In iterative synthetic training, causal detection of collapse undergoes a near-discontinuous onset at per-generation mixing ratio alpha_c approx 0.50 (sigmoid R^2=0.969). This detection phase transition is explained by competing signal strength and temporal resolution, predicting — and experimentally confirming — a non-monotonic dose-response including a synchronization dip at alpha approx 0.60.\"\n\n- **Title**: \"A Detection Phase Transition in Iterative Training Collapse\"\n\n- **Literature positioning**: Du et al. 是经验前辈但无统计检验框架; Scheffer et al. (2009/2012) 临界转变早期预警框架首次引入 model collapse; Gu et al. (2024, EMNLP, arXiv:2407.17467) data mixing; Gu et al. (2025, NeurIPS Spotlight, arXiv:2505.18091) data mixing phase transitions 提供理论类比; Truong & Truong (2025) universality classes; Calabrese (2003) hormesis 提供非单调 dose-response 跨学科框架; Deshpande (2010) zero-lag GC failure 解释 sync regime; Titus (2020) critical speeding up; Dakos et al. (2012) CSD methods。**核心区别**: 不是提出新方法，而是 **发现新现象** (detection phase transition) + **理论解释** (S x R model) + **实践启示** (taxonomy + early cessation + data strategy)。\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns & early-warning 框架引入 model collapse\"。讨论 rising autocorrelation/variance 类比 | 文献工作 |\n| \"双变量 Granger 无法排除混淆因子\" | token_entropy 和 ECE 可能共享信息源（均捕捉 output distribution shape），双变量 Granger 无法排除未观测混淆因子。论文 Discussion 须显式声明 Granger precedence ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），并讨论多变量 VAR 或条件 Granger 作为 future work。多方法一致性（4 种独立方法收敛）部分缓解但不消除此顾虑 | Discussion 声明 + future work |\n| **(D139)** \"Onset 锐度被高估\" | TY=7/8 at α=0.45 证明因果信号连续存在，PermG 0/8 是 power threshold 非 signal absence。fdr_p gradient (0.059-0.094) 在 sensitivity analysis 披露。\"steep onset\" 替代 \"near-discontinuous\"。Cross-seed raw variance (3/8 vs 6/8) 是 power boundary expected behavior。Skip α=0.47/0.48 (D140): T=20 更有价值 | a045_s43 TY (已有) |\n| **(D138)** \"CSD 证据形式化\" | Kendall τ p<0.001, canary 3/4 条件显著。CSD 定位为 C1 cascade mechanism evidence，非独立 claim。4-stage narrative arc 提供 mechanistic explanation | CSD v2 (已完成) |\n\n## Venue Fit\n\nEMNLP 2026 Main Track 优势：\n- NLP 社区对 model collapse / data contamination 高度关注，audience 精准\n- ACL/EMNLP 接受 empirical discovery + analysis paper，不要求 SOTA\n- Detection phase transition 是新发现（非 incremental methodology contribution）\n- S(alpha) x R(alpha) 竞争模型提供理论贡献，不只是经验观察\n- Iterative training / synthetic data 是 LLM 时代热点话题\n- 跨学科框架 (hormesis, zero-lag GC, CSD) 增加论文深度和 novelty\n\n风险：\n- \"phase transition 只是 sigmoid fit\" → 13-point alpha-sweep + cross-architecture validation + S x R 理论机制解释，非 curve fitting\n- 模型规模偏小 (Pythia-410M + GPT-2m) → 诚实标注 limitation + 10/14 cross-architecture + Pythia-6.9B + 1.4B scaling evidence\n- T=11 短时间序列统计效力 → T=20 验证 RUNNING + TY 降级 + PermG 作为主指标（不依赖 TY）\n- \"只有 2 个 alpha 点支撑 onset\" → alpha=0.45 (0/8) + alpha=0.50 (6/8) 是核心，但 alpha=0.25-0.45 五点全 0/8 + alpha=0.25 borderline p=0.053 提供 transition profile\n\n> **D130 Claim Mapping**: 下文约束中 \"Claim 1\" = 旧 Granger precedence (现属 C1/C3), \"Claim 2\" = 旧 regime-dependent (现属 C1/C2), \"Claim 3\" = 旧 method sensitivity (现属 C3), \"Claim 4\" = 旧 intervention (现属 C4)。约束内容仍然有效，只是贡献定位变了。\n\n## Key Narrative Constraints (from MVP + plan_003)\n\n1. **不能 claim \"canary predicts model collapse\"** — 只能 claim \"canary predicts diversity collapse\"。token_entropy→perplexity 经差分检验确认为伪回归（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99, D017），未差分时的\"弱信号\"完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动。论文措辞用\"spurious association confirmed by first-differencing\"，不再用\"弱信号\"\n2. **ECE→perplexity 和 token_entropy→perplexity 均为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后两者均 0/4 seed 显著。未差分时观察到的\"方向相反\"（token_entropy 领先 vs perplexity 领先 ECE）是共享趋势的不同表现形式。论文报告差分前后对比作为方法论教训，不再需要机制性解释\n3. **effective_rank 和 self_bleu 作为 canary 失败** — 必须全量报告，不能只报正面结果\n4. **distinct_3 必须放 supplementary，不能完全省略** — max_lag=7 下升为 SIGNIFICANT 但 lag_std>1.5（双峰：seed 42 总是 -5，其余 -1/-2），维持 D008 降级策略，主文聚焦 distinct_1/2。Supplementary 需给完整结果 + 解释 lag instability（高阶 n-gram diversity 对短序列随机变异更敏感）。distinct_3 的不稳定性本身支持 \"predictability boundary\" 主题——即使 diversity 维度内部也有可预测性梯度\n5. **max_lag=7 确认核心 lead 无截断** — token_entropy→distinct_1 lag=-1（std=0.0）、ECE→distinct_2 lag=-1（std=0.0）在 max_lag=5/7 下完全一致。token_entropy→perplexity lag=-5 虽非截断伪影，但已确认为非平稳伪回归（D017）\n6. **~~Dual-timescale 升级条件~~ — 已失效 (D017)** — perplexity 通道经差分确认为伪回归，即使 plan_006 T=20 也无法拯救伪信号。\"Two distinct lead horizons\" 升级路径关闭。plan_006 的价值重新定位为：(1) diversity lead 在更长序列中的稳定性，(2) TY power 改善观察，(3) 差分后 permutation Granger 在 T=20 下的表现\n7. **ECE→perplexity \"反向因果\"已确认为伪信号 (D017)** — 差分后 ECE→perplexity 0/4 SIG。之前的 post-hoc mechanistic hypothesis 无需讨论。论文只需报告差分前后对比\n8. **TY 在 N=11 下完全不可用于区分真/伪关联** — d_max sensitivity 分析证实：d_max=0 过度宽松（连 effective_rank 都通过），d_max=1 过度保守（连 token_entropy→distinct_1 都拒绝），adaptive 因 ADF 不稳定而跨 seed 震荡。论文**不能**用 TY 作为\"正确答案\"。**spurious_rate 必须双版本报告**：含 TY 75%（16 pair 中 12 仅 TY 判伪）、排除 TY 后 62.5%（16 pair 中 10 在 4 种非 TY 方法下一致）。ECE→distinct_2/3 被 TY 判伪但在其余 4 种方法全部 4/4 SIG——这本身是\"方法选择改变分类\"的例证（Claim 3 素材）\n9. **ECE 地位为 secondary canary — 原因升级为 bidirectionality (D063)** — ECE 前向 consensus 与 token_entropy 等效（PG_fwd 均 6/8），但反向 profile 分离：token_entropy reverse **0/28** across 7 conditions（完美单向因果），ECE reverse **13/28 significant, 5/7 conditions 有 ≥1 reverse pair**，全部为 distinct→ECE 方向。ECE 的 \"secondary\" 标签不再仅因 TY Type II error，而是基于 **genuine bidirectional coupling evidence**——ECE 与 diversity 存在双向反馈，因果解读受限。论文措辞：\"While both canaries achieve comparable forward consensus (PG_fwd=6/8), token_entropy shows no reverse significance (0/28), consistent with unidirectional causal precedence; ECE exhibits significant reverse coupling in 5/7 conditions (13/28 pairs), suggesting bidirectional feedback.\" 这同时服务 Claim 3：单看前向 sig 两者等价，加入反向检验后分离——分析维度选择改变结论\n10. **d_max sensitivity 曲线是论文的 key figure** — 三组 d_max（0/adaptive/1）× spurious rate 的变化是 Claim 3 的核心可视化，展示\"方法选择如何改变结论\"\n11. **统计效力必须显式论证** — Dream 三档一致 CRITICAL：T=10 下 TY 的 DOF 严重不足。论文必须包含 bootstrap/permutation 验证 + plan_006 (T=20) 结果。所有 p-value 必须经 BH FDR 校正\n12. **\"phase diagram\" → \"regime map\"** — 除非 α 分辨率达到 ≥6 个非平凡值 + 提供 α* uncertainty interval，否则用 \"predictability regime map\" 或 \"regime-dependent analysis\"。4-5 个 α 值不足以支撑物理学意义上的 \"phase diagram\"\n13. **Scheffer et al. 是必引文献** — critical slowing down / early-warning indicators 框架是本研究的知识祖先。论文 Related Work 必须引用并定位\n14. **CRS 降级为 illustration** — 不列入核心 contribution。CRS 是显然的反应式干预，Dream 三档一致认为 trivial/confounded\n15. **TY 正式降级为辅助指标 (D015)** — Bootstrap power analysis (N=1000 Monte Carlo): T=10 power=0.05-0.20, T=20 power=0.17, T=30 power=0.29 (d_max≥1)。核心分析框架改为 cross-correlation + permutation test + BH FDR 校正。TY 仅用于 Claim 3 展示（方法敏感性 + power analysis figure），不再作为 lead-lag 判定方法\n16. **Perplexity 通道确认为伪回归 (D017)** — Permutation Granger 差分版：canary→perplexity 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99)，canary→diversity 差分后 12/12 SIG（6 pair × 4 seed 中，token_entropy 和 ECE 各 3 pair 全通过）。差分前后结论翻转（perplexity: 6-8/8→0/8; diversity: 24/24→24/24）是 Claim 3 的关键 figure 素材。**F 值范围**：undifferenced F=74–3370, differenced F=71–7643（全 6 pairs × 4 seeds），范围重叠——之前\"F 不降反升\"仅对 token_entropy→distinct_1 子集成立，全量数据应以**显著性保持率 24/24→24/24**为核心叙事，不以 F 值大小为论据。论文 Method 节须注明：(1) T=11 first-difference 后 T=10，circular shift 仅 9 个唯一排列，null distribution 高度离散；(2) 尽管如此，diversity 通道 F 值远超任何排列值，结论不受离散性影响\n17. **f² 效应量报告约束 (D019 更新)** — 差分后 f²=102-435（token_entropy→distinct_1），因 df_resid=5-6 任何真实信号都产生极大 f²（RSS_unrestricted 趋近零）。论文**禁止**用 Cohen 的 0.02/0.15/0.35 基准解读这些 f² 值。正确做法：permutation p-value 为主要推断证据，f² 仅作\"效应存在\"的定性指示，不做定量比较。**代码层面**：当 df_resid < 20 时，分析脚本输出 `f2_size=\"df_too_low\"` 而非 small/medium/large 分类（D019 T3 已实现于 permutation_granger.py）\n18. **2×2 差分对比表是 Claim 3 核心 figure** — channel type（canary→diversity vs canary→perplexity）× differencing status（undifferenced vs differenced）的 2×2 表最紧凑展示\"一个方法论选择翻转整条通道的结论\"。diversity 通道差分前后均 24/24 SIG（显著性完全保持），F 值范围 undiff 74–3370 / diff 71–7643，部分 pair 略降但显著性完全保持。perplexity 通道差分后归零。比 d_max sensitivity 曲线更直观地展示差分的决定性影响\n19. **method_ablation 必须双版本报告 (D019)** — spurious_rate 报告含 TY（5-method: 75%）和排除 TY（4-method: 62.5%）两个数字。ECE→distinct_2/3 在 TY 下被判伪但在其余 4 种方法中 4/4 SIG——TY 的 Type II error 驱动的分类差异本身是 Claim 3 素材。论文主文用 4-method 数字作主要结论，5-method 数字用于 Claim 3 方法敏感性讨论\n20. **ECE canary 可靠性是 regime-dependent (D021)** — α=0.25 下 ECE gen 1 下降 -14%（反向信号），gen 2+ 才上升至 +77%。α=1.0 下 gen 1 直接 +2926%。ECE 作为早期预警 canary 仅在高 α（≥0.50，待 a050 确认）下可靠。低 α 下首代反向信号意味着 ECE 不能用于\"一代内预警\"。这丰富 Claim 2：canary 可靠性本身是 regime-dependent。D019 \"co-primary\" 限定为 \"high-α regime only\"\n21. **Claim 1 scope 确认为 α≥0.75 (D037, Director 批准)** — α=0.75 Permutation Granger FDR: 3/4 方向显著（ECE→d1 p_fdr=0.046, ECE→d2 p_fdr=0.046, entropy→d2 p_fdr=0.048），entropy→d1 边界 p_fdr=0.054。反向 4/4 全部不显著。Consensus 11/16 robust。**当前 Granger-causal precedence 在 α≥0.75 成立**（α=1.0: 24/24 FDR-sig 4 seeds; α=0.75: 3/4 FDR-sig single seed）。**Single-seed caveat (C001)**: α=0.75 仅 seed=42 验证，待 D026 multi-seed 确认后移除限定。**entropy→d1 borderline (p_fdr=0.054)**: 论文须讨论——3/4 显著 + 1/4 边界，不是 4/4 全过。α≤0.50 均 0/8 FDR-sig，detectability boundary 确认在 0.50→0.75 间\n22. **α=0.50 null 结果是 positive finding (D025, D037 更新)** — 中等污染下 canary 与 downstream 同步退化（temporal lead 消失），correlation/rule-based 方法（naive, differenced, threshold）100% sig vs 因果检验方法（permG, TY）0% sig 是 Claim 3（method × regime interaction）最极端的例证。论文呈现策略：(1) 此非单调 detectability 模式本身是 regime map 的核心特征；(2) **Detectability boundary 已确认在 0.50-0.75 区间 (D037)**——α=0.75 有 3/4 FDR-sig 信号，practical utility 定位为\"高污染场景（α≥0.75）下的早期预警\"，而非通用预警工具；(3) α=0.50 null 不是方法失败，是 signal-to-noise 的物理边界\n23. **α-sweep 全部 single seed 是当前最大弱点 (D026)** — 非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 -40.3% vs α=0.50 -37.3%）和 ECE regime switch 假说均基于 seed=42 的 point estimate，无置信区间。论文中非单调性结论**必须标注 \"observed at seed=42; multi-seed confirmation required\"**。multi-seed（α=0.25/0.50 各 2-3 seeds）是从 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要条件，优先于 finer α grid（a010/a060）。非单调性在主文仅报告现象（配 drift 表格），\"entropy floor\" 机制假说放 Discussion\n24. **α=0.25 双向 Granger 不改变 Claim 1 叙事 (D026)** — d1→entropy raw p=0.023（FDR 后 0.093 不显著），但效应量仅 f²=1.30 vs entropy→d1 的 f²=27.21（21 倍差异）。强单向因果 + 弱反馈，不是对称 coupled dynamics。α=1.0 反方向不显著，α=0.50 反方向 p=0.224。论文可加一句：\"marginally significant feedback at mild contamination (f²=1.30, 21× weaker than primary direction)\" 但不需要改写核心叙事\n25. **证据强度排序：Claim 3 > Claim 2 > Claim 1 (D026)** — Reviewer 独立评估。Claim 3（method sensitivity）最强且 novel、well-supported；Claim 2（regime-dependent）次之（但需 multi-seed）；Claim 1（Granger-causal）scope 最窄（仅 α≥0.75）。论文 framing 应确保 Claim 3 的方法论贡献获得足够篇幅，不因 Claim 1 的 \"canary prediction\" 叙事而被矮化\n26. **Phase 3 二次衰退仅限 Discussion，不纳入 Core Claims (D035)** — plan_006_s42 (T=18) 发现 gen 13-17 二次 diversity 衰退（d2 -5.8%, d3 -6.9%），两个硬约束：(a) 单 seed，plan_006_s43 确认前不写入论文主体；(b) Phase 3 中 token_entropy 反升 +2.8% 而 diversity 继续下降——canary 在长 horizon 下失效，升级为核心发现会成审稿攻击面。**最佳用法**：Claim 3 补充证据（观察窗口长度改变结论 = 方法敏感性例证）+ Discussion 段落（collapse dynamics richer than saturation, but canary utility diminishes at extended horizons）。不改变 Core Claims / narrative structure / 方法论主线\n27. **Consensus score 跨 α 比较须标注标准差异 (D038)** — α=0.75 consensus 11/16 robust 使用单种子 method-agreement 标准（≥4/5 方法判定显著），α=1.0 consensus 6/16 robust 使用 4-seed full consensus 标准（≥3/4 seeds × ≥4/5 方法）。两个数字不可直接对比——11/16 > 6/16 不意味着 α=0.75 信号更强，而是标准更宽松。论文中引用时**必须标注标准差异**，禁止直接对比暗示 α=1.0 信号更弱\n28. **token_entropy→perplexity 在 α=0.75 单种子 consensus 中的 ROBUST 标记不可引用 (D038)** — α=0.75 单种子 consensus 将 token_entropy→perplexity 标为 ROBUST（因多方法一致通过），但 D017 已用 4-seed 差分检验确认该方向为 spurious（自相关驱动，差分后 0/4 sig）。这是单种子 consensus 的已知局限——无法检测共享非平稳趋势导致的伪关联。论文中**不得引用该方向作为 α=0.75 的 robust evidence**，仅限 canary→diversity 方向\n29. **D075 Canary 盲区 (α=0.10) + MMCT v2 fp32 双 seed 权威确认** — 10% 合成数据污染下，canary（entropy/ECE）与 0% null 不可区分（entropy drift -4.6~-5.0%），但 diversity 退化达 d1=-23.0%（fp32 双 seed 均值）。**MMCT v2 = 0/8**（uniform_forward + wglobal 跨双 seed 一致，置换检验 + null 校准）。Raw PermG = 2-3/8（参数敏感），但 MMCT v2 null 校准后归零——**raw 检出不超过 null 期望**。**论文 framing 精确化**：说 \"not significantly above null\" 而非 \"no detection\"（raw 有散发检出但不超 null 范围）。wpair 在 α≤0.10 不稳定（仅 3 null seeds，FPR 权重方差大），论文用 uniform/wglobal 作权威值。**论文定位**：(a) canary 检测存在统计下界，honest limitation；(b) diversity 对微量污染的脆弱性是独立发现——5-10% 污染 diversity 已大幅受损但 canary 无统计显著信号；(c) 丰富 Claim 2 regime map\n30. **D079+D093+D094 bf16 效应 regime+scale dependent + padding_side + 6.9B 最终结论** — **高 α (≥0.50) 小模型 (410m)**: bf16 stochastic rounding 抑制 collapse（entropy 5.2×差异, ppl ~730×差异）。**低 α (0.10) 小模型**: bf16 collapse 反而更重（bf16 s43 d1=-29.1% vs fp32 s43 d1=-22.1%）。**大模型 (6.9B) α=1.0 bf16 完整 10 代 (D094+D095)**: entropy -56%, d1 -25.3%, ppl +2749%。Canary lead 复现（Gen1 entropy -40%, d1 -1%）。**D095 修正**：6.9B K≥4=6/8 与 410M fp32 K≥4=6/8 完全一致（PermG 7/8 vs 6/8 略强，diff 6/8 vs 8/8 略弱），论文 framing 为 **\"signal persists at 17× scale\"** 非 \"strengthens\"（Agent 报告的 410M K≥4=2/8 为错误数据 M024）。PPL +2749% 远超 410m bf16 +68.6%——bf16 正则化效应随规模递减。**MAUVE 地板效应**：6.9B Gen0 mauve=0.030（410M 通常 >0.80），全程 <0.032——排除出 Table 7 scale 行。**3 个 caveat**：(a) bf16 与 scale 效应混淆，论文标注 \"bf16; scale vs precision confounded\"；(b) 单 seed；(c) fp32 6.9B 可行（~54GB < 98GB GPU）但 ~18h 非 P0。**D093 padding_side**：所有实验 generation 阶段 right-padding bug 一致影响，相对比较有效但高 α 不成比例受影响（累积效应与 synthetic ratio 成正比）。代码已修复，robustness check 待做。**硬约束**：Table 7 + dose-response 主轴只用 fp32；≤3B fp32 强制；6.9B bf16 单独讨论\n31. **D085 Perplexity 在极端 collapse 下丧失 lag structure** — α=1.00 permG 检出率 75% (24/32) 低于 α=0.75 的 83% (20/24)。8/32 non-sig 全集中在 canary→perplexity 对，4 seeds 一致（p 0.35-0.99）。根因非 BH 校正伪影（0 次 BH 翻转），是 perplexity 时序在极端 collapse 下变为近单调爆炸增长，破坏 permG 的 lag structure。distinct_N 对跨全部 seed 100%。**论文贡献**：(a) 支持推荐 diversity 类指标而非 perplexity 作为 downstream；(b) 丰富 Claim 3（downstream 指标选择也是方法论敏感性的一个维度）\n32. **MMCT 正式命名** — 多方法一致性共识框架正式命名为 **Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT)**。null-calibrated（FPR=0 at α=0.00），加权方案（FPR-inverse weighting），检出率 43.8%/58.3%/79.2% at α=0.25/0.50/0.75。在论文 Method 节正式定义，Results 节展示其优于单方法的 sensitivity-specificity tradeoff\n33. **Confident diffusion 机制 (scale_14b, Discussion 材料)** — Pythia-1.4B α=0.50 effective_rank +62%（gen_0→1 阶跃后 plateau），与 410M 的倒 U 型 (+27%) 对比为 scale-amplified phenomenon。机制假说：collapse 使模型输出分布从结构化/有峰转为扁平/扩散到更多 embedding 维度。仅限 Discussion 段落，不纳入 Core Claims（泛化性待跨架构验证）\n34. **Constraint #12 升级：8 个 α 值已满足 \"regime map\" 门槛** — 当前有 8 个非平凡 α 值（0.00/0.05/0.10/0.25/0.50/0.60/0.75/1.00），超过约束 #12 的 ≥6 阈值。论文中可使用 \"predictability regime map\"。如果 α=0.05 和 Current text:
```
\paragraph{Predictions for longer generation chains.}
The $S \times R$ framework (\S\ref{sec:theory}) generates testable predictions for longer observation windows ($T{=}20$).
Increased $T$ improves temporal resolution without changing signal strength, effectively increasing $R(\alpha)$ by providing more observation points for Granger tests to detect sub-generation lags.


```

I'm now articulating the three specific predictions that follow from this framework: at the weakest effect size, we expect detection to improve with more data; at the intermediate level, finer temporal resolution should reveal previously masked lags and eliminate the synchronous dip; and at the strongest effect size, the signal is already clear so additional power won't change much. These predictions are testable against the new $T{=}20$ experiments.

But I need to recalibrate this against the power theory from D133—the critical threshold $\alpha_c$ should actually shift leftward as we accumulate more generations, not stay fixed. The transition between detection regimes should be smoother rather than sharp, and different methods like PermG and MMCT will have their own distinct critical thresholds. I should tie this back to the power interpretation section in the methods to make the theoretical grounding explicit.

I'm also realizing the contribution order in the introduction needs restructuring. The phase transition finding should be elevated to the primary contribution, with the S×R theory and PermG recommendation as the second pillar, then the multi-method framework as supporting infrastructure, and finally the practical applications for early detection and stopping rules.

Let me start by fixing the experiments section to align with the power theory, then verify whether CCM appears anywhere in the main text before deciding on its treatment.
````

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/docs/paper/experiments.tex",
  "old_string": "\\paragraph{Predictions for longer generation chains.}\nThe $S \\times R$ framework (\\S\\ref{sec:theory}) generates testable predictions for longer observation windows ($T{=}20$).\nIncreased $T$ improves temporal resolution without changing signal strength, effectively increasing $R(\\alpha)$ by providing more observation points for Granger tests to detect sub-generation lags.\nWe predict: (i)~at $\\alpha{=}0.50$, PermG detection should increase from 6/8 to ${\\geq}7/8$ as additional generations provide more statistical power for an already-present lead;\n(ii)~at $\\alpha{=}0.60$, the synchronous dip should attenuate or disappear, because finer temporal sampling allows Granger tests to resolve lags that are invisible at $T{=}11$;\n(iii)~at $\\alpha{=}1.0$, detection should remain at 6--8/8, as the strong signal is already saturated.\nThese predictions are submitted for future empirical verification with extended generation protocols.",
  "new_string": "\\paragraph{Predictions for longer generation chains.}\nThe statistical power interpretation (\\S\\ref{sec:theory}) generates three testable predictions for $T{=}20$.\nSince $\\mathrm{Power}(\\alpha, T) = \\Phi\\bigl(S(\\alpha) \\cdot R(\\alpha) \\cdot \\sqrt{T} \\,/\\, \\sigma_{\\mathrm{noise}}\\bigr)$, increasing $T$ raises power at every $\\alpha$:\n(i)~$\\alpha_c$ should \\emph{left-shift}---the detection threshold decreases because $\\sqrt{20}/\\sqrt{10} \\approx 1.41$ multiplies the power argument uniformly, so contamination levels currently below threshold (e.g., $\\alpha{=}0.45$, where $T{=}10$ yields PermG 0/8 despite TY 7/8) may cross into detectability;\n(ii)~the transition should \\emph{smooth}---the $\\sqrt{T}$ scaling broadens the power curve across $\\alpha$, making the onset less step-like (the synchronous dip at $\\alpha{=}0.60$ should also attenuate as finer temporal sampling resolves sub-generation lags);\n(iii)~different methods should exhibit \\emph{different} $\\alpha_c$ values, since each method has its own power curve---the existing dissociation at $\\alpha{=}0.45$ (PermG 0/8 vs.\\ TY 7/8) already demonstrates method-dependent detection thresholds (\\S\\ref{sec:theory}), and $T{=}20$ should make these separations more pronounced.\nThese predictions are currently under empirical verification with extended ($T{=}20$) generation protocols."
}
```

  "old_string": "Yet the statistical methodology underlying these lead-lag claims has received little scrutiny.\nWhether a candidate canary genuinely \\emph{precedes} downstream degradation, or merely correlates with it under shared non-stationary trends, is a question that existing analyses leave unresolved.\nIterative training produces short, noisy time series (often fewer than 15 points per generation sequence), where detection power drops sharply and false positive rates can be high~\\cite{boettiger2012limits}.\nIn the absence of proper controls, the choice of statistical method alone can shift conclusions on the same data from universal significance to complete null.\nThis makes the validity of existing canary claims an open question with direct consequences for practitioners who rely on such signals to trigger training interventions.",
  "new_string": "Yet a fundamental question remains unanswered: \\emph{at what contamination level does causal detection of collapse become possible?}\nWhether a candidate canary genuinely \\emph{precedes} downstream degradation---or merely correlates with it under shared non-stationary trends---depends on contamination regime and statistical method in ways that existing analyses leave unresolved.\nIterative training produces short, noisy time series (often fewer than 15 points per generation sequence), where detection power drops sharply and false positive rates can be high~\\cite{boettiger2012limits}.\nIn the absence of proper controls, the choice of statistical method alone can shift conclusions on the same data from universal significance to complete null.\nThis makes both the validity and the regime-dependence of existing canary claims open questions with direct consequences for practitioners who rely on such signals to trigger training interventions."
}

<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — Edit · `toolu_017RzLuidpYiVzUod4Cfh1S5`</summary>

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/docs/paper/introduction.tex",
  "old_string": "We address these gaps through a multiverse-style analysis~\\cite{bell2022modeling,kapoor2024reforms} of canary prediction of diversity collapse, the progressive loss of lexical diversity (as measured by distinct-$n$~\\cite{li2016diversity}) across training generations.\nUsing Pythia-410M~\\cite{biderman2023pythia} across six contamination levels ($\\alpha \\in \\{0.00, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00\\}$) and up to four random seeds, we apply five statistical methods to expose three methodological pitfalls (method sensitivity, spurious regression, and regime dependence) through the Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT), a formal FPR-inverse-weighted consensus framework inspired by specification curve analysis~\\cite{simonsohn2020specification}.\nAn extended eight-point regime map ($\\alpha \\in \\{0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 1.00\\}$) reveals a canary blind spot: at $\\alpha{=}0.10$, diversity has already collapsed measurably but canary metrics remain at null levels, which places a detection lower bound at $\\alpha{\\geq}0.50$.\nPermutation Granger causality emerges as the only method with both zero null-control FPR and high sensitivity; applied to token entropy, it identifies a robust one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse, and early detection enables timely cessation of synthetic-data training, reducing peak perplexity by 58\\% compared to reactive cessation at $\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$.\nCross-architecture validation on GPT-2-medium and OPT-1.3B confirms that these findings transfer beyond the primary experimental platform.\nGranger precedence in our framework establishes temporal predictive improvement, not true causality~\\cite{shojaie2022granger}; our canary claim is scoped to diversity metrics, not model collapse broadly.\nTo our knowledge, this constitutes the first multiverse-style analysis of lead-lag claims in model collapse: the same dataset, analyzed with five methods across six contamination regimes, yields conclusions that span the full spectrum from universal significance to complete null.",
  "new_string": "We discover a \\emph{detection phase transition} in canary-based monitoring of iterative collapse.\nUsing Pythia-410M~\\cite{biderman2023pythia} across eight contamination levels ($\\alpha \\in \\{0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 1.00\\}$) and up to four random seeds, we show that causal detectability undergoes a steep onset at per-generation mixing ratio $\\alpha_c{=}0.476$ (sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$): permutation Granger causality (PermG) jumps from 0/8 FDR-significant metric pairs at $\\alpha{\\leq}0.45$ to 6/8 at $\\alpha{=}0.50$, despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination across the boundary.\nA signal-times-resolution ($S \\times R$) framework explains this onset and the non-monotonic dose-response (including a synchronous dip at $\\alpha{\\approx}0.60$) through competing signal strength and temporal resolution.\nThe discovery is enabled by a multi-method consensus framework (MMCT)~\\cite{bell2022modeling,kapoor2024reforms} comparing five statistical methods, which reveals that method choice alone can shift conclusions on the same data from universal significance to complete null---the same dataset, analyzed with five methods across six contamination regimes, yields conclusions spanning the full spectrum.\nCross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$) in 12 cases (86\\%).\nGranger precedence in our framework establishes temporal predictive improvement, not true causality~\\cite{shojaie2022granger}; our canary claim is scoped to diversity metrics (distinct-$n$~\\cite{li2016diversity}), not model collapse broadly."
}
```

the most liberal method yielding 75\\% forward-direction FPR even at the null ($\\alpha{=}0.0$).\n    We identify three pitfalls (method sensitivity, spurious regression under shared trends, and null-control FPR with high sensitivity, exhibiting a detection phase transition ($\\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$): 0/8 through $\\alpha{=}0.45$, near-discontinuous onset at $\\alpha{=}0.50$ (6/8, dual-seed), a non-monotonic dip at $\\alpha{=}0.60$ (synchronous collapse), and reliable detection at $\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$.\n    Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$ metric pairs) in 12 cases (86\\%), with directional asymmetry (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse) confirmed across four We apply five lead-lag detection methods to the same data and expose three methodological pitfalls: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\\% (inter-method $\\kappa{=}0.185$), first-differencing eliminates canary$\\to$perplexity associations entirely ($F{\\approx}0$, $p{=}0.99$), and regime dependence causes the same Granger test to shift from 20/24 FDR-significant pairs at $\\alpha{=}0.75$ to 0/24 at $\\alpha{\\leq}0.25$.\n    The Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT) formalizes this comparison through FPR-inverse weighted consensus with null-calibrated $p$-values.\n    Shapley-value decomposition (exact 32-coalition enumeration; Figure~\\ref{fig:shapley}) identifies naive cross-correlation as the sole FPR driver (Shapley(FPR)${=}{+}0.348$) and permutation Granger as the strongest suppressor (Shapley(FPR)${=}{-}0.194$).\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Permutation Granger causality as the recommended detection method} (\\S\\ref{sec:case_study}, \\S\\ref{sec:generalization}).\n    Among the five methods evaluated, permutation Granger causality (PermG) is the only one combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity.\n    PermG identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse: on WikiText-103, 24/24 forward-direction pairs are FDR-significant and 0/24 in the reverse direction at $\\alpha{=}1.0$ (4 seeds $\\times$ 6 metric pairs), confirmed across GPT-2-medium, OPT-1.3B, and four additional model families.\n    Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$) in 12 cases (86\\%).\n    At $\\alpha{=}0.75$, three-seed replication yields 20/24 forward and 0/24 reverse.\n    This claim is scoped to diversity metrics (distinct-$n$); the perplexity channel is spurious.\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Dose-response characterization revealing a detection phase transition} (\\S\\ref{sec:regime}).\n    A signal-times-resolution framework (\\S\\ref{sec:theory}) predicts non-monotonic detectability; PermG confirms a four-regime structure: 0/8 through $\\alpha{=}0.45$ 6/8 per seed at $\\alpha{=}1.0$, four seeds).\n    Sigmoid regression quantifies the onset as a detection phase transition ($\\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$), occurring despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination across the transition boundary.\n    At $\\alpha{=}0.10$, a canary blind spot emerges: diversity has already collapsed (distinct-1 drops 39.6\\%) but canary metrics remain indistinguishable from the null condition (entropy drift $-5.3\\%$ vs.\\ null $-4.6{\\pm}0.4\\%$, a gap of less than one percentage point).\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Canary-based early detection and cessation} (\\S\\ref{sec:intervention}).\n    As a practical demonstration, we compare predictive cessation (triggered when token entropy drops ${>}15\\%$) against reactive cessation (triggered when distinct-1 drops ${>}10\\%$).\n    The entropy-based trigger fires one generation earlier, enabling cessation of synthetic-data training before irreversible damage: peak perplexity is reduced by 58\\% (pooled estimate; bootstrap 95\\% CI: 29--64\\%)\\footnote{Bootstrap CI computed from two seed-precision pairs (s42 fp32, s43 bf16); the interval reflects observed variation rather than population-level uncertainty.} and convergence completes one generation faster (Generation~2 vs.\\ Generation~3).\n    At $\\alpha{=}0.75$, entropy drops 31.6\\% at generation~1, exceeding the 15\\% threshold and confirming that the trigger generalizes beyond full synthetic replacement.\n    Results are validated at $\\alpha{=}1.0$ across two model scales: Pythia-410M (s42 fp32; s43 bf16, subject to precision effects discussed in \\S\\ref{sec:discussion}) and Pythia-1.4B (s42 fp32), confirming that the trigger and recovery pattern is scale-independent.\n\\end{enumerate}",

String: Our contributions are:
\begin{enumerate}
    \item \textbf{Systematic multi-method evaluation framework} (\S\ref{sec:method}, \S\ref{sec:method_sensitivity}).
    We apply five lead-lag detection methods to the same data and expose three methodological pitfalls: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\% (inter-method $\kappa{=}0.185$), with the most liberal method yielding 75\% forward-direction FPR even at the null ($\alpha{=}0.0$).
    We identify three pitfalls (method sensitivity, spurious regression under shared trends, and regime dependence) and systematically compare five statistical methods through a Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT).
    Permutation Granger causality emerges as the only method combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity, exhibiting a detection phase transition ($\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\Delta\alpha{\approx}0.02$): 0/8 through $\alpha{=}0.45$, near-discontinuous onset at $\alpha{=}0.50$ (6/8, dual-seed), a non-monotonic dip at $\alpha{=}0.60$ (synchronous collapse), and reliable detection at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$.
    Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations confirms PermG detection (${\geq}6/8$ metric pairs) in 12 cases (86\%), with directional asymmetry (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse) confirmed across four seeds on WikiText-103.
    First-differencing cleanly separates genuine from spurious channels: all canary$\to$diversity leads survive while all canary$\to$perplexity leads vanish.
    Applied to Pythia-410M and replicated across six model families (345M--6.9B parameters), the framework identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse, with canary-based predictive cessation validated across two model scales (410M, 1.4B).

    \item \textbf{Systematic multi-method evaluation framework} (\S\ref{sec:method}, \S\ref{sec:method_sensitivity}).
    We apply five lead-lag detection methods to the same data and expose three methodological pitfalls: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\% (inter-method $\kappa{=}0.185$), first-differencing eliminates canary$\to$perplexity associations entirely ($F{\approx}0$, $p{=}0.99$), and regime dependence causes the same Granger test to shift from 20/24 FDR-significant pairs at $\alpha{=}0.75$ to 0/24 at $\alpha{\leq}0.25$.
    The Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT) formalizes this comparison through FPR-inverse weighted consensus with null-calibrated $p$-values.
    Shapley-value decomposition (exact 32-coalition enumeration; Figure~\ref{fig:shapley}) identifies naive cross-correlation as the sole FPR driver (Shapley(FPR)${=}{+}0.348$) and permutation Granger as the strongest suppressor (Shapley(FPR)${=}{-}0.194$).

    \item \textbf{Permutation Granger causality as the recommended detection method} (\S\ref{sec:case_study}, \S\ref{sec:generalization}).
    Among the five methods evaluated, permutation Granger causality (PermG) is the only one combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity.
    PermG identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse: on WikiText-103, 24/24 forward-direction pairs are FDR-significant and 0/24 in the reverse direction at $\alpha{=}1.0$ (4 seeds $\times$ 6 metric pairs), confirmed across GPT-2-medium, OPT-1.3B, and four additional model families.
    Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\geq}6/8$) in 12 cases (86\%).
    At $\alpha{=}0.75$, three-seed replication yields 20/24 forward and 0/24 reverse.
    This claim is scoped to diversity metrics (distinct-$n$); the perplexity channel is spurious.

    \item \textbf{Dose-response characterization revealing a detection phase transition} (\S\ref{sec:regime}).
    A signal-times-resolution framework (\S\ref{sec:theory}) predicts non-monotonic detectability; PermG confirms a four-regime structure: 0/8 through $\alpha{=}0.45$ (blind), near-discontinuous onset at $\alpha{=}0.50$ (6/8, dual-seed), a synchronous dip at $\alpha{=}0.60$ (0--3/8), and reliable detection at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$ (5--6/8 per seed, three seeds; 6/8 per seed at $\alpha{=}1.0$, four seeds).
    Sigmoid regression quantifies the onset as a detection phase transition ($\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\Delta\alpha{\approx}0.02$), occurring despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination across the transition boundary.
    At $\alpha{=}0.10$, a canary blind spot emerges: diversity has already collapsed (distinct-1 drops 39.6\%) but canary metrics remain indistinguishable from the null condition (entropy drift $-5.3\%$ vs.\ null $-4.6{\pm}0.4\%$, a gap of less than one percentage point).

    \item \textbf{Canary-based early detection and cessation} (\S\ref{sec:intervention}).
    As a practical demonstration, we compare predictive cessation (triggered when token entropy drops ${>}15\%$) against reactive cessation (triggered when distinct-1 drops ${>}10\%$).
    The entropy-based trigger fires one generation earlier, enabling cessation of synthetic-data training before irreversible damage: peak perplexity is reduced by 58\% (pooled estimate; bootstrap 95\% CI: 29--64\%)\footnote{Bootstrap CI computed from two seed-precision pairs (s42 fp32, s43 bf16); the interval reflects observed variation rather than population-level uncertainty.} and convergence completes one generation faster (Generation~2 vs.\ Generation~3).
    At $\alpha{=}0.75$, entropy drops 31.6\% at generation~1, exceeding the 15\% threshold and confirming that the trigger generalizes beyond full synthetic replacement.
    Results are validated at $\alpha{=}1.0$ across two model scales: Pythia-410M (s42 fp32; s43 bf16, subject to precision effects discussed in \S\ref{sec:discussion}) and Pythia-1.4B (s42 fp32), confirming that the trigger and recovery pattern is scale-independent.
\end{enumerate}&lt;/tool_use_error>

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_013XDgNBdbnaxM6jUrPhKVfp`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	When language models train iteratively on their own output, the training distribution progressively loses its tail modes, a phenomenon termed model collapse~\cite{shumailov2024curse,alemohammad2024mad}.
5	Lexical diversity narrows, calibration deteriorates, and each generation inherits the systematic biases of its predecessors.
6	As machine-generated text becomes prevalent on the web, such feedback loops pose a growing and often invisible risk to production training pipelines.
7	By the time standard quality metrics register degradation, the underlying distribution may have already shifted irreversibly.
8	This threat motivates the search for cheap-to-compute \emph{canary metrics}: leading indicators that signal distributional degradation before expensive downstream measures reveal the full extent of collapse.
9	The concept parallels early-warning signals for critical transitions in ecological systems~\cite{scheffer2009early,scheffer2012anticipating}, where proxy indicators allow intervention before a tipping point.
10	Several recent studies have pursued this direction: \citet{du2026geometric} observe that calibration error rises before accuracy degrades, SIGMA~\cite{sigma2026spectral} proposes spectral indicators, and DCES~\cite{dces2026counter} uses center-edge sampling dynamics as a collapse signal.
11	
12	Yet a fundamental question remains unanswered: \emph{at what contamination level does causal detection of collapse become possible?}
13	Whether a candidate canary genuinely \emph{precedes} downstream degradation---or merely correlates with it under shared non-stationary trends---depends on contamination regime and statistical method in ways that existing analyses leave unresolved.
14	Iterative training produces short, noisy time series (often fewer than 15 points per generation sequence), where detection power drops sharply and false positive rates can be high~\cite{boettiger2012limits}.
15	In the absence of proper controls, the choice of statistical method alone can shift conclusions on the same data from universal significance to complete null.
16	This makes both the validity and the regime-dependence of existing canary claims open questions with direct consequences for practitioners who rely on such signals to trigger training interventions.
17	
18	We identify four specific deficiencies that pervade existing lead-lag analyses of iterative training collapse.
19	(1)~No differencing tests rule out spurious regression from shared non-stationary trends, a well-documented hazard in analyses of co-trending time series~\cite{granger1974spurious}.
20	(2)~Conclusions rest on a single statistical method whose assumptions about stationarity and directionality are rarely examined; no multi-method cross-validation is conducted.
21	(3)~No false discovery rate (FDR) correction (e.g., Benjamini--Hochberg) guards against spurious findings when testing many metric pairs simultaneously.
22	(4)~No regime-dependent analysis is performed, despite the possibility that the same test yields opposite conclusions at different contamination levels.
23	The closest predecessor is \citet{du2026geometric}, who report that calibration error rises before accuracy degrades in iteratively trained language models.
24	Their analysis, however, covers a single metric pair, spans only 1--3 generations, and relies on visual comparisons without statistical tests.
25	The early-warning framework of \citet{scheffer2009early,scheffer2012anticipating} provides theoretical motivation but has not been applied to training collapse with the rigor that such short, non-stationary series require.
26	
27	We discover a \emph{detection phase transition} in canary-based monitoring of iterative collapse.
28	Using Pythia-410M~\cite{biderman2023pythia} across eight contamination levels ($\alpha \in \{0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 1.00\}$) and up to four random seeds, we show that causal detectability undergoes a steep onset at per-generation mixing ratio $\alpha_c{=}0.476$ (sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\Delta\alpha{\approx}0.02$): permutation Granger causality (PermG) jumps from 0/8 FDR-significant metric pairs at $\alpha{\leq}0.45$ to 6/8 at $\alpha{=}0.50$, despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination across the boundary.
29	A signal-times-resolution ($S \times R$) framework explains this onset and the non-monotonic dose-response (including a synchronous dip at $\alpha{\approx}0.60$) through competing signal strength and temporal resolution.
30	The discovery is enabled by a multi-method consensus framework (MMCT)~\cite{bell2022modeling,kapoor2024reforms} comparing five statistical methods, which reveals that method choice alone can shift conclusions on the same data from universal significance to complete null---the same dataset, analyzed with five methods across six contamination regimes, yields conclusions spanning the full spectrum.
31	Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\geq}6/8$) in 12 cases (86\%).
32	Granger precedence in our framework establishes temporal predictive improvement, not true causality~\cite{shojaie2022granger}; our canary claim is scoped to diversity metrics (distinct-$n$~\cite{li2016diversity}), not model collapse broadly.
33	
34	Our contributions are:
35	\begin{enumerate}
36	    \item \textbf{Systematic multi-method evaluation framework} (\S\ref{sec:method}, \S\ref{sec:method_sensitivity}).
37	    We apply five lead-lag detection methods to the same data and expose three methodological pitfalls: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\% (inter-method $\kappa{=}0.185$), first-differencing eliminates canary$\to$perplexity associations entirely ($F{\approx}0$, $p{=}0.99$), and regime dependence causes the same Granger test to shift from 20/24 FDR-significant pairs at $\alpha{=}0.75$ to 0/24 at $\alpha{\leq}0.25$.
38	    The Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT) formalizes this comparison through FPR-inverse weighted consensus with null-calibrated $p$-values.
39	    Shapley-value decomposition (exact 32-coalition enumeration; Figure~\ref{fig:shapley}) identifies naive cross-correlation as the sole FPR driver (Shapley(FPR)${=}{+}0.348$) and permutation Granger as the strongest suppressor (Shapley(FPR)${=}{-}0.194$).
40	
41	    \item \textbf{Permutation Granger causality as the recommended detection method} (\S\ref{sec:case_study}, \S\ref{sec:generalization}).
42	    Among the five methods evaluated, permutation Granger causality (PermG) is the only one combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity.
43	    PermG identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse: on WikiText-103, 24/24 forward-direction pairs are FDR-significant and 0/24 in the reverse direction at $\alpha{=}1.0$ (4 seeds $\times$ 6 metric pairs), confirmed across GPT-2-medium, OPT-1.3B, and four additional model families.
44	    Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\geq}6/8$) in 12 cases (86\%).
45	    At $\alpha{=}0.75$, three-seed replication yields 20/24 forward and 0/24 reverse.
46	    This claim is scoped to diversity metrics (distinct-$n$); the perplexity channel is spurious.
47	
48	    \item \textbf{Dose-response characterization revealing a detection phase transition} (\S\ref{sec:regime}).
49	    A signal-times-resolution framework (\S\ref{sec:theory}) predicts non-monotonic detectability; PermG confirms a four-regime structure: 0/8 through $\alpha{=}0.45$ (blind), near-discontinuous onset at $\alpha{=}0.50$ (6/8, dual-seed), a synchronous dip at $\alpha{=}0.60$ (0--3/8), and reliable detection at $\alpha{\geq}0.75$ (5--6/8 per seed, three seeds; 6/8 per seed at $\alpha{=}1.0$, four seeds).
50	    Sigmoid regression quantifies the onset as a detection phase transition ($\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\Delta\alpha{\approx}0.02$), occurring despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination across the transition boundary.
51	    At $\alpha{=}0.10$, a canary blind spot emerges: diversity has already collapsed (distinct-1 drops 39.6\%) but canary metrics remain indistinguishable from the null condition (entropy drift $-5.3\%$ vs.\ null $-4.6{\pm}0.4\%$, a gap of less than one percentage point).
52	
53	    \item \textbf{Canary-based early detection and cessation} (\S\ref{sec:intervention}).
54	    As a practical demonstration, we compare predictive cessation (triggered when token entropy drops ${>}15\%$) against reactive cessation (triggered when distinct-1 drops ${>}10\%$).
55	    The entropy-based trigger fires one generation earlier, enabling cessation of synthetic-data training before irreversible damage: peak perplexity is reduced by 58\% (pooled estimate; bootstrap 95\% CI: 29--64\%)\footnote{Bootstrap CI computed from two seed-precision pairs (s42 fp32, s43 bf16); the interval reflects observed variation rather than population-level uncertainty.} and convergence completes one generation faster (Generation~2 vs.\ Generation~3).
56	    At $\alpha{=}0.75$, entropy drops 31.6\% at generation~1, exceeding the 15\% threshold and confirming that the trigger generalizes beyond full synthetic replacement.
57	    Results are validated at $\alpha{=}1.0$ across two model scales: Pythia-410M (s42 fp32; s43 bf16, subject to precision effects discussed in \S\ref{sec:discussion}) and Pythia-1.4B (s42 fp32), confirming that the trigger and recovery pattern is scale-independent.
58	\end{enumerate}
59	
60	The remainder of this paper is organized as follows.
61	\S\ref{sec:related_work} surveys related work on model collapse, early-warning signals, and lead-lag methodology.
62	\S\ref{sec:background} establishes the formal setup, notation, and the five detection methods used throughout.
63	\S\ref{sec:method} distills the three methodological pitfalls into a unified diagnostic framework, presents the multi-method consensus pipeline, and reports its empirical calibration.
64	\S\ref{sec:experiments} provides full experimental results: method sensitivity analysis (\S\ref{sec:method_sensitivity}), regime-dependent detectability (\S\ref{sec:regime}), canary prediction of diversity collapse (\S\ref{sec:case_study}), consensus framework validation (\S\ref{sec:consensus_validation}), cross-architecture generalization (\S\ref{sec:generalization}), and canary-based early intervention (\S\ref{sec:intervention}).
65	\S\ref{sec:discussion} discusses implications and future directions, and \S\ref{sec:conclusion} concludes, followed by a dedicated Limitations section (\S\ref{sec:limitations}).
66	
```

</details>

regression quantifies the onset as a detection phase transition ($\\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$), occurring despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination across the transition boundary.\n    At $\\alpha{=}0.10$, a canary blind spot emerges: diversity has already collapsed (distinct-1 drops 39.6\\%) but canary metrics remain indistinguishable from the null condition (entropy drift $-5.3\\%$ vs.\\ null $-4.6{\\pm}0.4\\%$, a gap of less than one percentage point).\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Canary-based early detection and cessation} (\\S\\ref{sec:intervention}).\n    As a practical demonstration, we compare predictive cessation (triggered when token entropy drops ${>}15\\%$) against reactive cessation (triggered when distinct-1 drops ${>}10\\%$).\n    The entropy-based trigger fires one generation earlier, enabling cessation of synthetic-data training before irreversible damage: peak perplexity is reduced by 58\\% (pooled estimate; bootstrap 95\\% CI: 29--64\\%)\\footnote{Bootstrap CI computed from two seed-precision pairs (s42 fp32, s43 bf16); the interval reflects observed variation rather than population-level uncertainty.} and convergence completes one generation faster (Generation~2 vs.\\ Generation~3).\n    At $\\alpha{=}0.75$, entropy drops 31.6\\% at generation~1, exceeding the 15\\% threshold and confirming that the trigger generalizes beyond full synthetic replacement.\n    Results are validated at $\\alpha{=}1.0$ across two model scales: Pythia-410M (s42 fp32; s43 bf16, subject to precision effects discussed in \\S\\ref{sec:discussion}) and Pythia-1.4B (s42 fp32), confirming that the trigger and recovery pattern is scale-independent.\n\\end{enumerate}\n\nThe remainder of this paper is organized as follows.\n\\S\\ref{sec:related_work} surveys related work on model collapse, early-warning signals, and lead-lag methodology.\n\\S\\ref{sec:background} establishes the formal setup, notation, and the five detection methods used throughout.\n\\S\\ref{sec:method} distills the three methodological pitfalls into a unified diagnostic framework, presents the multi-method consensus pipeline, and reports its empirical calibration.\n\\S\\ref{sec:experiments} provides full experimental results: method sensitivity analysis (\\S\\ref{sec:method_sensitivity}), regime-dependent detectability (\\S\\ref{sec:regime}), canary prediction of diversity collapse (\\S\\ref{sec:case_study}), consensus framework validation (\\S\\ref{sec:consensus_validation}), cross-architecture generalization (\\S\\ref{sec:generalization}), and canary-based early intervention (\\S\\ref{sec:intervention}).\n\\S\\ref{sec:discussion} discusses implications and future directions, and \\S\\ref{sec:conclusion} concludes, followed by a dedicated Limitations section (\\S\\ref{sec:limitations}).\n",
      "numLines": 66,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 66
    }

drift $-5.3\\%$ vs.\\ null $-4.6{\\pm}0.4\\%$).\n    Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B) confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$) in 12 cases (86\\%), with directional asymmetry (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse) confirmed across four seeds on WikiText-103.\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Signal-times-resolution theory} (\\S\\ref{sec:theory}).\n    A $S(\\alpha) \\times R(\\alpha)$ framework predicts the non-monotonic dose-response: signal strength $S$ increases monotonically with $\\alpha$, but temporal resolution $R$ drops at $\\alpha{\\approx}0.60$ when relaxation time matches the observation interval.\n    A statistical power interpretation formalizes the onset as a power threshold---$\\mathrm{Power}(\\alpha, T) = \\Phi\\bigl(S \\cdot R \\cdot \\sqrt{T}/\\sigma\\bigr)$---generating three testable predictions for $T{=}20$: left-shift of $\\alpha_c$, transition smoothing, and method-dependent detection thresholds.\n    The existing dissociation at $\\alpha{=}0.45$ (PermG 0/8 vs.\\ Toda--Yamamoto 7/8) already confirms method-dependent $\\alpha_c$.\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Multi-method diagnostic toolkit} (\\S\\ref{sec:method}, \\S\\ref{sec:method_sensitivity}).\n    The Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT), comparing five lead-lag methods through FPR-inverse weighted consensus, serves as the instrument enabling the above discovery.\n    It exposes three methodological pitfalls: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\\% (inter-method $\\kappa{=}0.185$), first-differencing eliminates canary$\\to$perplexity associations entirely ($F{\\approx}0$, $p{=}0.99$) while preserving all canary$\\to$diversity leads (24/24$\\to$24/24), and regime dependence causes the same Granger test to shift from 20/24 FDR-significant at $\\alpha{=}0.75$ to 0/24 at $\\alpha{\\leq}0.25$.\n    PermG emerges as the only method combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity, identifying token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse at $\\alpha{=}1.0$, four seeds).\n\n    \\item \\textbf{Canary-based early detection and cessation} (\\S\\ref{sec:intervention}).\n    Predictive cessation (triggered when token entropy drops ${>}15\\%$) fires one generation earlier than reactive cessation, reducing peak perplexity by 58\\% (pooled estimate; bootstrap 95\\% CI: 29--64\\%)\\footnote{Bootstrap CI computed from two seed-precision pairs (s42 fp32, s43 bf16); the interval reflects observed variation rather than population-level uncertainty.} and enabling convergence one generation faster.\n    A four-layer detection taxonomy---dimensionality collapse ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.10$), distributional variance ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.25$), causal coupling ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.50$), dynamical stability ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.50$)---provides practitioners with regime-appropriate diagnostic tools.\n    Results are validated across two model scales (Pythia-410M, Pythia-1.4B), confirming scale-independent trigger and recovery patterns.\n\\end{enumerate}",

    "oldString": "\\begin{abstract}\nWe show that lead-lag conclusions in iterative model collapse are fragile: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\\% (inter-method $\\kappa{=}0.091$--$0.185$), with the most liberal method yielding 75\\% forward-direction FPR even at the null ($\\alpha{=}0.0$).\nWe identify three pitfalls (method sensitivity, spurious regression under shared trends, and regime dependence) and systematically compare five statistical methods through a Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT).\nPermutation Granger causality emerges as the only method combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity, exhibiting a detection phase transition ($\\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$): 0/8 through $\\alpha{=}0.45$, near-discontinuous onset at $\\alpha{=}0.50$ (6/8, dual-seed), a non-monotonic dip at $\\alpha{=}0.60$ (synchronous collapse), and reliable detection at $\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$.\nCross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$ metric pairs) in 12 cases (86\\%), with directional asymmetry (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse) confirmed across four seeds on WikiText-103.\nFirst-differencing cleanly separates genuine from spurious channels: all canary$\\to$diversity leads survive while all canary$\\to$perplexity leads vanish.\nApplied to Pythia-410M and replicated across six model families (345M--6.9B parameters), the framework identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse, with canary-based predictive cessation validated across two model scales (410M, 1.4B).\n\\end{abstract}",
    "newString": "\\begin{abstract}\nWe discover a \\emph{detection phase transition} in canary-based monitoring of iterative training collapse: causal detectability undergoes a steep onset at per-generation mixing ratio $\\alpha_c{=}0.476$ (sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$), with permutation Granger causality (PermG) jumping from 0/8 FDR-significant metric pairs at $\\alpha{\\leq}0.45$ to 6/8 at $\\alpha{=}0.50$ (dual-seed), despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination.\nA non-monotonic dose-response---blind ($\\alpha{\\leq}0.45$), onset ($\\alpha{\\approx}0.50$), synchronous dip ($\\alpha{\\approx}0.60$), detection ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$)---is explained by a signal-times-resolution ($S \\times R$) framework: signal strength rises monotonically with $\\alpha$, but temporal resolution drops when relaxation time matches the observation interval.\nA statistical power interpretation predicts three testable consequences for longer generation chains ($T{=}20$): left-shift of $\\alpha_c$, transition smoothing, and method-dependent detection thresholds.\nThe discovery is enabled by a multi-method consensus framework (MMCT) comparing five statistical methods, which reveals that method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to 62.5\\% and first-differencing eliminates all canary$\\to$perplexity associations while preserving canary$\\to$diversity leads (24/24$\\to$24/24).\nPermG is the only method combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity; applied to Pythia-410M and validated across 14 model-seed combinations spanning six families (345M--6.9B), it identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse at $\\alpha{=}1.0$).\nCanary-based predictive cessation reduces peak perplexity by 58\\% compared to reactive cessation.\n\\end{abstract}",
    "originalFile": "\\begin{abstract}\nWe show that lead-lag conclusions in iterative model collapse are fragile: method choice alone inflates spurious-signal rates to at least 62.5\\% (inter-method $\\kappa{=}0.091$--$0.185$), with the most liberal method yielding 75\\% forward-direction FPR even at the null ($\\alpha{=}0.0$).\nWe identify three pitfalls (method sensitivity, spurious regression under shared trends, and regime dependence) and systematically compare five statistical methods through a Multi-Method Consensus Test (MMCT).\nPermutation Granger causality emerges as the only method combining zero null-control FPR with high sensitivity, exhibiting a detection phase transition ($\\alpha_c{=}0.476$, sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$): 0/8 through $\\alpha{=}0.45$, near-discontinuous onset at $\\alpha{=}0.50$ (6/8, dual-seed), a non-monotonic dip at $\\alpha{=}0.60$ (synchronous collapse), and reliable detection at $\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$.\nCross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$ metric pairs) in 12 cases (86\\%), with directional asymmetry (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse) confirmed across four seeds on WikiText-103.\nFirst-differencing cleanly separates genuine from spurious channels: all canary$\\to$diversity leads survive while all canary$\\to$perplexity leads vanish.\nApplied to Pythia-410M and replicated across six model families (345M--6.9B parameters), the framework identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse, with canary-based predictive cessation validated across two model scales (410M, 1.4B).\n\\end{abstract}",

          "-Cross-architecture validation across 14 model-seed combinations confirms PermG detection (${\\geq}6/8$ metric pairs) in 12 cases (86\\%), with directional asymmetry (24/24 forward, 0/24 reverse) confirmed across four seeds on WikiText-103.",
          "-First-differencing cleanly separates genuine from spurious channels: all canary$\\to$diversity leads survive while all canary$\\to$perplexity leads vanish.",
          "-Applied to Pythia-410M and replicated across six model families (345M--6.9B parameters), the framework identifies token entropy as a one-generation-ahead canary for diversity collapse, with canary-based predictive cessation validated across two model scales (410M, 1.4B).",
          "+We discover a \\emph{detection phase transition} in canary-based monitoring of iterative training collapse: causal detectability undergoes a steep onset at per-generation mixing ratio $\\alpha_c{=}0.476$ (sigmoid $R^2{=}0.969$, $\\Delta\\alpha{\\approx}0.02$), with permutation Granger causality (PermG) jumping from 0/8 FDR-significant metric pairs at $\\alpha{\\leq}0.45$ to 6/8 at $\\alpha{=}0.50$ (dual-seed), despite only 0.15 percentage points difference in cumulative contamination.",
          "+A non-monotonic dose-response---blind ($\\alpha{\\leq}0.45$), onset ($\\alpha{\\approx}0.50$), synchronous dip ($\\alpha{\\approx}0.60$), detection ($\\alpha{\\geq}0.75$)---is explained by a signal-times-resolution ($S \\times R$) framework: signal strength rises monotonically with $\\alpha$, but temporal resolution drops when relaxation time matches the observation interval.",
          "+A statistical power interpretation predicts three testable consequences for longer generation chains ($T{=}20$): left-shift of $\\alpha_c$, transition smoothing, and method-dependent detection thresholds.",
